import time
from collections import OrderedDict


class LRUCache:
    """Fixed-capacity least-recently-used cache with optional per-entry TTL.

    Entries are kept in an ``OrderedDict`` ordered from least to most recently
    used, so every operation is O(1) on average.

    TTL semantics:

    * The TTL is measured with ``clock`` and starts when an entry is written
      by ``put``. Reads do not extend it; updating a key restarts it.
    * An entry written at time ``t`` is live while ``clock() < t + ttl`` and
      expired from ``t + ttl`` on.
    * Expiry is lazy. An expired entry is dropped (and counted once in
      ``expirations``) when ``get``, ``__contains__`` or ``delete`` touches it,
      or when it is evicted for capacity. ``__len__`` is O(1), so it counts
      stored entries and can include expired ones that nobody has touched yet.

    The cache is not thread-safe.
    """

    def __init__(self, capacity, ttl=None, clock=time.monotonic):
        if capacity <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"capacity must be positive, got {capacity!r}")
        self._capacity = capacity
        self._ttl = ttl
        self._clock = clock
        # key -> (value, expires_at); expires_at is None when there is no TTL.
        self._data = OrderedDict()
        self._hits = 0
        self._misses = 0
        self._evictions = 0
        self._expirations = 0

    def _live_entry(self, key):
        """Return the entry for ``key`` or None, dropping it if it expired.

        Never changes recency.
        """
        entry = self._data.get(key)
        if entry is None:
            return None
        expires_at = entry[1]
        if expires_at is not None and self._clock() >= expires_at:
            del self._data[key]
            self._expirations += 1
            return None
        return entry

    def get(self, key, default=None):
        entry = self._live_entry(key)
        if entry is None:
            self._misses += 1
            return default
        self._data.move_to_end(key)
        self._hits += 1
        return entry[0]

    def put(self, key, value):
        expires_at = None if self._ttl is None else self._clock() + self._ttl
        self._data[key] = (value, expires_at)
        self._data.move_to_end(key)
        if len(self._data) > self._capacity:
            self._data.popitem(last=False)
            self._evictions += 1

    def delete(self, key):
        """Remove ``key``; return True only if a live entry was removed."""
        if self._live_entry(key) is None:
            return False
        del self._data[key]
        return True

    def stats(self):
        return {
            "hits": self._hits,
            "misses": self._misses,
            "evictions": self._evictions,
            "expirations": self._expirations,
        }

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self._data)

    def __contains__(self, key):
        return self._live_entry(key) is not None

+import inspect
+import random
+import time
+
+import pytest
+
+from cachelib import LRUCache
+
+
+class FakeClock:
+    def __init__(self, now=1000.0):
+        self.now = now
+        self.calls = 0
+
+    def __call__(self):
+        self.calls += 1
+        return self.now
+
+    def advance(self, seconds):
+        self.now += seconds
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def clock():
+    return FakeClock()
+
+
+def stats_of(**expected):
+    base = {"hits": 0, "misses": 0, "evictions": 0, "expirations": 0}
+    base.update(expected)
+    return base
+
+
+class TestConstruction:
+    @pytest.mark.parametrize("capacity", [0, -1, -100])
+    def test_non_positive_capacity_raises(self, capacity):
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+            LRUCache(capacity)
+
+    def test_non_positive_capacity_raises_with_ttl(self):
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+            LRUCache(0, ttl=5)
+
+    def test_default_clock_is_monotonic(self):
+        param = inspect.signature(LRUCache).parameters["clock"]
+        assert param.default is time.monotonic
+
+    def test_default_ttl_is_none(self):
+        assert inspect.signature(LRUCache).parameters["ttl"].default is None
+
+    def test_starts_empty_with_zero_stats(self):
+        cache = LRUCache(3)
+        assert len(cache) == 0
+        assert cache.stats() == stats_of()
+
+    def test_real_clock_ttl_smoke(self):
+        cache = LRUCache(2, ttl=60)
+        cache.put("a", 1)
+        assert cache.get("a") == 1
+        assert "a" in cache
+
+
+class TestBasicOperations:
+    def test_put_then_get(self):
+        cache = LRUCache(2)
+        cache.put("a", 1)
+        assert cache.get("a") == 1
+
+    def test_get_missing_returns_default(self):
+        cache = LRUCache(2)
+        assert cache.get("nope") is None
+        assert cache.get("nope", "fallback") == "fallback"

Reads do not extend it; updating a key restarts it.\n    * An entry written at time ``t`` is live while ``clock() < t + ttl`` and\n      expired from ``t + ttl`` on.\n    * Expiry is lazy. An expired entry is dropped (and counted once in\n      ``expirations``) when ``get``, ``__contains__`` or ``delete`` touches it,\n      or when it is evicted for capacity. ``__len__`` is O(1), so it counts\n      stored entries and can include expired ones that nobody has touched yet.\n\n    The cache is not thread-safe.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, capacity, ttl=None, clock=time.monotonic):\n        if capacity <= 0:\n            raise ValueError(f\"capacity must be positive, got {capacity!r}\")\n        self._capacity = capacity\n        self._ttl = ttl\n        self._clock = clock\n        # key -> (value, expires_at); expires_at is None when there is no TTL.\n        self._data = OrderedDict()\n        self._hits = 0\n        self._misses = 0\n        self._evictions = 0\n        self._expirations = 0\n\n    def _live_entry(self, key):\n        \"\"\"Return the entry for ``key`` or None, dropping it if it expired.\n\n        Never changes recency.\n        \"\"\"\n        entry = self._data.get(key)\n        if entry is None:\n            return None\n        expires_at = entry[1]\n        if expires_at is not None and self._clock() >= expires_at:\n            del self._data[key]\n            self._expirations += 1\n            return None\n        return entry\n\n    def get(self, key, default=None):\n        entry = self._live_entry(key)\n        if entry is None:\n            self._misses += 1\n            return default\n        self._data.move_to_end(key)\n        self._hits += 1\n        return entry[0]\n\n    def put(self, key, value):\n        expires_at = None if self._ttl is None else self._clock() + self._ttl\n        self._data[key] = (value, expires_at)\n        self._data.move_to_end(key)\n        if len(self._data) > self._capacity:\n            self._data.popitem(last=False)\n            self._evictions += 1\n\n    def delete(self, key):\n        \"\"\"Remove ``key``; return True only if a live entry was removed.\"\"\"\n        if self._live_entry(key) is None:\n            return False\n        del self._data[key]\n        return True\n\n    def stats(self):\n        return {\n            \"hits\": self._hits,\n            \"misses\": self._misses,\n            \"evictions\": self._evictions,\n            \"expirations\": self._expirations,\n        }\n\n    def __len__(self):\n        return len(self._data)\n\n    def __contains__(self, key):\n        return self._live_entry(key) is not None\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3971,"cache_read_input_tokens":27140,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3971},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6d8cfb7-a47c-4aba-976d-dea551afbb47","uuid":"183c7c91-59de-4330-b110-5feaea054366","timestamp":"2026-10-12T18:07:17.941Z","request_id":"req_011CfxndSwnVhe8dQzWBE1Mg","wire_tool_inputs":{"toolu_013MPubZ2qd5KcrrHatnH4xw":{"file_path":"/home/alustosa/git/model-routing-eval/.work/agent-eval/effort-demo-sonnet-5-5-xhigh/cases/t04_lru_cache/cachelib/lru.py","content":"import time\nfrom collections import OrderedDict\n\n\nclass LRUCache:\n    \"\"\"Fixed-capacity least-recently-used cache with optional per-entry TTL.\n\n    Entries are kept in an ``OrderedDict`` ordered from least to most recently\n    used, so every operation is O(1) on average.\n\n    TTL semantics:\n\n    * The TTL is measured with ``clock`` and starts when an entry is written\n      by ``put``.   item = self.items.pop(i)\n        self.items.append(item)\n        self.counters[\"hits\"] += 1\n        return item[1]\n\n    def put(self, key, value):\n        expires_at = None if self.ttl is a list ordered from LRU to MRU.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, capacity, ttl, clock):\n        self.capacity = capacity\n        self.ttl = ttl\n        self.clock = clock\n        self.items = []  # [key, value, expires_at]\n        self.counters = {\"hits\": 0, \"misses\": 0, \"evictions\": 0, \"expirations\": 0}\n\n    def _find(self, key):\n        for i, item in enumerate(self.items):\n            if item[0] == key:\n                return i\n        return None\n\n    def _live_index(self, key):\n        i = self._find(key)\n        if i is None:\n            return None\n        expires_at = self.items[i][2]\n        if expires_at is not None and self.clock.now >= expires_at:\n            del self.items[i]\n            self.counters[\"expirations\"] += 1\n            return None\n        return i\n\n    def get(self, key, default=None):\n        i = self._live_index(key)\n        if i is None:\n            self.counters[\"misses\"] += 1\n            return default\n        item = self.items.pop(i)\n        self.items.append(item)\n        self.counters[\"hits\"] += 1\n        return item[1]\n\n    def put(self, key, value):\n        expires_at = None if self.ttl is None else self.clock.now + self.ttl\n        i = self._find(key)\n        if i is not None:\n            del self.items[i]\n        self.items.append([key, value, expires_at])\n        if len(self.items) > self.capacity:\n            del self.items[0]\n            self.counters[\"evictions\"] += 1\n\n    def delete(self, key):\n        i = self._live_index(key)\n        if i is None:\n            return False\n        del self.items[i]\n        return True\n\n    def contains(self, key):\n        return self._live_index(key) is not None\n\n    def __len__(self):\n        return len(self.items)\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"seed\", range(20))\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"ttl\", [None, 5])\ndef test_random_operations_match_reference_model(seed, ttl):\n    rng = a list ordered from LRU to MRU.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, capacity, ttl, clock):\n        self.capacity = capacity\n        self.ttl = ttl\n        self.clock = clock\n        self.items = []  # [key, value, expires_at]\n        self.counters = {\"hits\": 0, \"misses\": 0, \"evictions\": 0, \"expirations\": 0}\n\n    def _find(self, key):\n        for i, item in enumerate(self.items):\n            if item[0] == key:\n                return i\n        return None\n\n    def _live_index(self, key):\n        i = self._find(key)\n        if i is None:\n            return None\n None\n        return i\n\n    def get(self, key, default=None):\n        i = self._live_index(key)\n        if i is None:\n            self.counters[\"misses\"] += 1\n            return default\n        item = self.items.pop(i)\n        self.items.append(item)\n        self.counters[\"hits\"] += 1\n        return item[1]\n\n    def put(self, key, value):\n        expires_at = None if self.ttl is None else self.clock.now + self.ttl\n        i = self._find(key)\n        if i is not None:\n            del self.items[i]\n        self.items.append([key, value, expires_at])\n        if len(self.items) > self.capacity:\n            del self.items[0]\n            self.counters[\"evictions\"] += 1\n\n    def delete(self, key):\n        i = self._live_index(key)\n        if i is None:\n            return False\n        del self.items[i]\n        return True\n\n    def contains(self, key):\n        return self._live_index(key) is not None\n\n    def __len__(self):\n        return len(self.items)\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"seed\", range(20))\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"ttl\", [None, 5])\ndef test_random_operations_match_reference_model(seed, ttl):\n    rng = clock):\n        self.capacity = capacity\n        self.ttl = ttl\n        self.clock = clock\n        self.items = []  # [key, value, expires_at]\n        self.counters = {\"hits\": 0, \"misses\": 0, \"evictions\": 0, \"expirations\": 0}\n\n    def _find(self, key):\n        for i, item in enumerate(self.items):\n            if item[0] == key:\n                return i\n        return None\n\n    def _live_index(self, key):\n        i = self._find(key)\n        if i is None:\n            return None\n        expires_at = self.items[i][2]\n        if expires_at is not None and self.clock.now >= expires_at:\n            del self.items[i]\n            self.counters[\"expirations\"] += 1\n            return None\n        return i\n\n    def get(self, key, default=None):\n        i = self._live_index(key)\n        if i is None:\n            self.counters[\"misses\"] += 1\n            return default\n        item = self.items.pop(i)\n        self.items.append(item)\n        self.counters[\"hits\"] += 1\n        return item[1]\n\n    def put(self, key, value):\n        expires_at = None if self.ttl is None else self.clock.now + self.ttl\n        i = self._find(key)\n        if i is not None:\n            del self.items[i]\n        self.items.append([key, value, expires_at])\n        if len(self.items) > self.capacity:\n            del self.items[0]\n            self.counters[\"evictions\"] += 1\n\n    def delete(self, key):\n        i = self._live_index(key)\n        if i is None:\n            return False\n        del self.items[i]\n        return True\n\n rng.randint(1, 6)\n    cache = LRUCache(capacity, ttl=ttl, clock=clock)\n    model = _Reference(capacity, ttl, clock)\n    keys = list(range(10))\n\n    for step in range(400):\n        op = import inspect
import random
import time

import pytest

from cachelib import LRUCache


class FakeClock:
    def __init__(self, now=1000.0):
        self.now = now
        self.calls = 0

    def __call__(self):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.now

    def advance(self, seconds):
        self.now += seconds


@pytest.fixture
def clock():
    return FakeClock()


def stats_of(**expected):
    base = {"hits": 0, "misses": 0, "evictions": 0, "expirations": 0}
    base.update(expected)
    return base


class TestConstruction:
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("capacity", [0, -1, -100])
    def test_non_positive_capacity_raises(self, capacity):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            LRUCache(capacity)

    def test_non_positive_capacity_raises_with_ttl(self):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            LRUCache(0, ttl=5)

    def test_default_clock_is_monotonic(self):
        param = inspect.signature(LRUCache).parameters["clock"]
        assert param.default is time.monotonic

    def test_default_ttl_is_none(self):
        assert inspect.signature(LRUCache).parameters["ttl"].default is None

    def test_starts_empty_with_zero_stats(self):
        cache = LRUCache(3)
        assert len(cache) == 0
        assert cache.stats() == stats_of()

    def test_real_clock_ttl_smoke(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2, ttl=60)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        assert cache.get("a") == 1
        assert "a" in cache


class TestBasicOperations:
    def test_put_then_get(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        assert cache.get("a") == 1

    def test_get_missing_returns_default(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        assert cache.get("nope") is None
        assert cache.get("nope", "fallback") == "fallback"

    def test_stored_none_is_a_hit_not_the_default(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        cache.put("a", None)
        assert cache.get("a", "fallback") is None
        assert cache.stats()["hits"] == 1
        assert cache.stats()["misses"] == 0

    def test_falsy_values_round_trip(self):
        cache = LRUCache(4)
        for key, value in [("zero", 0), ("empty", ""), ("false", False), ("list", [])]:
            cache.put(key, value)
        assert cache.get("zero", "d") == 0
        assert cache.get("empty", "d") == ""
        assert cache.get("false", "d") is False
        assert cache.get("list", "d") == []

    def test_update_replaces_value_without_growing(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        cache.put("a", 2)
        assert len(cache) == 1
        assert cache.get("a") == 2

    def test_various_hashable_keys(self):
        cache = LRUCache(5)
        keys = [1, "1", (1, 2), None, 1.5]
        for i, key in enumerate(keys):
            cache.put(key, i)
        for i, key in enumerate(keys):
            assert cache.get(key) == i

    def test_unhashable_key_raises_type_error(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        with pytest.raises(TypeError):
            cache.put([1], "x")


class TestEviction:
    def test_evicts_least_recently_used(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        cache.put("b", 2)
        cache.put("c", 3)
        assert "a" not in cache
        assert cache.get("b") == 2
        assert cache.get("c") == 3
        assert len(cache) == 2

    def test_get_marks_most_recently_used(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        cache.put("b", 2)
        cache.get("a")
        cache.put("c", 3)
        assert "b" not in cache
        assert "a" in cache and "c" in cache

    def test_failed_get_does_not_change_recency(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        cache.put("b", 2)
        cache.get("zzz")
        cache.put("c", 3)
        assert "a" not in cache

    def test_update_marks_most_recently_used(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        cache.put("b", 2)
        cache.put("a", 10)
        cache.put("c", 3)
        assert "b" not in cache
        assert cache.get("a") == 10

    def test_update_of_existing_key_never_evicts(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        cache.put("b", 2)
        cache.put("b", 3)
        cache.put("a", 4)
        assert len(cache) == 2
        assert cache.stats()["evictions"] == 0

    def test_capacity_one(self):
        cache = LRUCache(1)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        cache.put("b", 2)
        assert "a" not in cache
        assert cache.get("b") == 2
        cache.put("b", 3)
        assert cache.get("b") == 3
        assert cache.stats()["evictions"] == 1

    def test_eviction_order_follows_access_pattern(self):
        cache = LRUCache(3)
        for key in "abc":
            cache.put(key, key)
        cache.get("a")  # order, oldest first: b c a
        cache.put("d", "d")  # evicts b
        cache.get("c")  # order: a d c
        cache.put("e", "e")  # evicts a
        assert "b" not in cache
        assert "a" not in cache
        assert all(k in cache for k in "cde")
        assert cache.stats()["evictions"] == 2

    def test_len_never_exceeds_capacity(self):
        cache = LRUCache(5)
        for i in range(100):
            cache.put(i, i)
            assert len(cache) <= 5
        assert len(cache) == 5
        assert cache.stats()["evictions"] == 95


class TestDelete:
    def test_delete_existing_returns_true_and_removes(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        assert cache.delete("a") is True
        assert "a" not in cache
        assert len(cache) == 0

    def test_delete_missing_returns_false(self):
        cache = LRUCache(2)
        assert cache.delete("a") is False
